#### 📋 STRY-0628-SPRITE-SHEET-GEN: 🛡️ SOVEREIGNOS: WILDCARD ADVOCATE GENERATOR BATCH SPRITE-SHEET PIPELINE
*   **System ID:** `d23d2d4ee7f444749ce987bfb3238e0f`
*   **Full Description:**
> ﻿# 🛡️ SOVEREIGNOS: WILDCARD ADVOCATE GENERATOR BATCH SPRITE-SHEET PIPELINE
> 
> 
> **Ticket ID:** STRY-0628-SPRITE-SHEET-GEN (WO-2026-099)  
> **Priority:** ⚡ P1 — High-Priority Operations  
> **Assigned To:** `antigravity`  
> **Ecosystem Location:** Sovereign Wildcard Suite (Port 3020, `SovereignOS/18_BarbStack/src/components/WildcardAdvocateGenerator.tsx`)  
> **Verification Node:** Clio (100.73.155.70)  
> **Parent Sprint:** Sovereign Media Production & Asset Ingress (Sprint ID 3)
> 
> 
> ---
> 
> 
> ## 📌 I. THE PILOT'S DIRECTIVE & WORKFLOW OVERVIEW
> 
> 
> Yo! We are standardizing our image generation pipeline inside the **Wildcard Advocate Generator** to eliminate visual asset drift. Currently, generating standalone advocate avatar cards across different states and historical eras forces multiple individual prompt generations, leading to inconsistent facial structures and broken character continuity.
> 
> 
> This work order directs the implementation of the **Batch Sprite-Sheet Pipeline** by leveraging the multi-state canvas grid standard established in `metsy_collar.png` and the `CAT-ALPHA` structural blueprint. The system will compile a single, high-density $3 \times 3$ sprite-sheet grid for any active advocate in a single image generation pass. By locking the character's base/anchor facial structure on the left side, the engine automatically wraps its generated pixels around a strict spatial matrix, shifting uniforms across time (Modern, 1970s, 1920s) and emotional expressions across columns (Regular, Excited, Disgruntled).
> 
> 
> ---
> 
> 
> ## ⚙️ II. TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS & SYSTEM ARCHITECTURE
> 
> 
> Antigravity must integrate the following features into the Wildcard Generator workspace:
> 
> 
> ### 1. The Left Selection Anchor Panel (The Core Roster Loop)
> *   Deploy an active roster list on the left-hand panel of the Wildcard UI.
> *   The system dynamically scans `sovereign_personas_export.md` / `m2m_persona_room` and displays the base 1x1 profile avatar of every active advocate (e.g. `@barf`, `@420_linda`, `@7_train_terry`).
> *   Allow multi-select batch queueing.
> 
> 
> ### 2. The 3x3 Sprite-Sheet Matrix Engine
> The generator wraps the selected base facial structure and compiles a single, high-density canvas structured as an exact $3 \times 3$ grid:
> *   **Row 1: Modern Era ($Y_1$):**
>     *   *Col 1 (Regular):* Base profile headshot in modern brand apparel.
>     *   *Col 2 (Excited):* High-vibe, victory-state expression.
>     *   *Col 3 (Disgruntled):* Meltdown/stress reaction mode.
> *   **Row 2: 1970s Throwback ($Y_2$):**
>     *   *Col 1 (Regular):* 1970s retro sideburns/hair and pullover racing-stripe Mets/Phillies uniform.
>     *   *Col 2 (Excited):* Vintage cap fly-off/arms-waving animation state.
>     *   *Col 3 (Disgruntled):* Throwing a cardboard stadium soda cup, hands on head.
> *   **Row 3: 1920s Throwback ($Y_3$):**
>     *   *Col 1 (Regular):* Sepia/monochrome tones, baggy wool uniform, newsboy fan cap.
>     *   *Col 2 (Excited):* Shouting through a vintage megaphone, waving newspaper.
>     *   *Col 3 (Disgruntled):* Scowling behind a wire-mesh backstop, skewed flat cap.
> 
> 
> ### 3. Cross-Tenant Slicing & Campaign Selectors
> *   **The Sprite Cutting Engine**: Once the master canvas is generated, the frontend slices the coordinates based on precise grid percentages, automatically saving the nine separate state assets into our persistent media library.
> *   **The Campaign Selector**: Support a dropdown option to hot-swap the thematic grid presets: selecting `[Theme: Beach Promotion]` or `[Theme: Golf Tournament]` instantly updates the row constraints to generate themed promotional assets (e.g. holding Wildseed products or sitting by the water) for corporate marketing loops.
> 
> 
> ---
> 
> 
> ## 🔒 III. SECURITY, CONCURRENCY & ROBUSTNESS SAFEGUARDS
> 
> 
> 1.  **BOM Preservation Safeguard:** The ingestion engine (`organize_inbox.py`) must parse and store this complete, high-fidelity Markdown body of the work order within the database's unified ticket attachments table (`sys_attachment`) to prevent metadata clipping.
> 2.  **No Core Schema Mutations:** Do not alter the core `sys_user` relational tables. Save the generated sliced assets as distinct references inside the media registry linked to the parent persona ID.
> 3.  **Low-Latency Render Cache:** Sliced sprite textures must be cached locally to ensure that swapping an advocate's expression during a live game occurs with zero delay on your 55-inch display monitor.
> 
> 
> ---
> 
>

#### 📋 STRY-0628-PROMPT-PREVIEW: 🛡️ SOVEREIGNOS: VERTEX PROMPT PAYLOAD INSPECTION CONSOLE & INTERCEPTOR
*   **System ID:** `eca7c414c9cc4665a6d6629afaf4cb76`
*   **Full Description:**
> ﻿# 🛡️ SOVEREIGNOS: VERTEX PROMPT PAYLOAD INSPECTION CONSOLE & INTERCEPTOR
> 
> 
> **Ticket ID:** STRY-0628-PROMPT-PREVIEW (WO-2026-098)  
> **Priority:** ⚡ P1 — High-Priority Operations  
> **Assigned To:** `antigravity`  
> **Ecosystem Location:** Sovereign Sports Cockpit / Vertex Inflow Engine (Port 3016, `SovereignOS/01_Sovereign_Portal/src/components/PromptPreviewConsole.tsx`)  
> **Verification Node:** Clio (100.73.155.70)  
> **Parent Sprint:** Sovereign Engineering Quality & Standards (Sprint ID 3)
> 
> 
> ---
> 
> 
> ## 📌 I. THE PILOT'S DIRECTIVE & INCIDENT OVERVIEW
> 
> 
> Yo, Pilot! We are diagnosing a major semantic convergence issue in the active watch party rooms. Following the hotfix for venue-awareness filters (`WO-2026-094`), our advocate chatbot swarm—specifically `@barf`—has lost its classic, unhinged comedic edge. Because the venue-awareness rule is applied globally as a hard constraint, the Vertex API is over-correcting to satisfy the prompt's physical location demands (spamming "OUR HOUSE" or "CITI FIELD" in every line), completely diluting and crushing unique character temperament traits.
> 
> 
> To prevent these silent prompt quality regressions, we need absolute visibility. This work order directs the implementation of the **Vertex Prompt Payload Inspection Console** by retrofitting our existing schema preview and multi-tenant state serialization UI. This console will intercept the fully compiled, raw prompt payload string *directly preceding* its delivery to the downstream Vertex API endpoint, allowing you to review, edit, and fine-tune system weights, token balances, and character directives live from your laptop or phone before execution.
> 
> 
> ---
> 
> 
> ## ⚙️ II. TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS & UI PLAYBOOK
> 
> 
> Antigravity must compile the following React and backend modifications:
> 
> 
> ### 1. Payload Interceptor Middleware (`prompt_interceptor.py`)
> *   Implement an API interceptor middleware layer in the FastAPI backend (`sdlc_portal_server.py` / `fanstack_relay.py`).
> *   Instead of blindly shipping compiled JSONs to Vertex, catch the payload, log it to an in-memory transactional buffer, and broadcast the state down a secure WebSocket channel to Port 3016.
> *   Support a manual block/bypass toggle: if `intercept_mode` is enabled, pause API delivery and wait for a `POST /api/prompt/release` event from the preview panel.
> 
> 
> ### 2. High-Density Inspection Dashboard (`PromptPreviewConsole.tsx`)
> Create a responsive, dual-column React workspace layout:
> *   **Split Ratios:** 35% Left Sidebar (Configuration & Metadata), 65% Right Workspace (Raw Payload Editor).
> *   **The Left Sidebar (35% Width):** Displays a compact, list-style summary of the active CMDB state context:
>     *   Target Persona (e.g., `@barf` [Premium]).
>     *   Active Game Telemetry (e.g. `PHI @ NYM`, `Bases Loaded`).
>     *   Injected System Filters (e.g. `Location: Home`, `Decorum Limit: OFF`).
>     *   Token Weight Gauges (Personality: 85% vs. Venue Inflation: 15%).
> *   **The Right Workspace (65% Width):** Renders a high-density, monospaced text area containing the full compiled raw prompt body (with system roles, behavioral instructions, and context rules). Features active text-highlighting for injected constraints (such as the "Citi Field" venue filter).
> *   **Action Toggles:** Center-aligned bottom button cluster featuring `[ RELEASE TO VERTEX ]` in cyan-500 and `[ HARD OVERRIDE PROMPT ]` in orange-600.
> 
> 
> ---
> 
> 
> ## 🔒 III. SECURITY, CONCURRENCY & ROBUSTNESS SAFEGUARDS
> 
> 
> 1.  **BOM Preservation Safeguard:** The ingestion engine (`organize_inbox.py`) must parse and store this complete, high-fidelity Markdown body of the work order within the database's unified ticket attachments table (`sys_attachment`) to prevent metadata clipping.
> 2.  **Zero Hallucinated Success:** The preview console operates as a pure stateless observer. Under no circumstances will the interface predict or fake backend completions.
> 3.  **Fail-Safe Pass-Through:** If the interceptor console loses connection or experiences socket timeouts, the API must automatically fall back to standard non-blocking pass-through mode to prevent live chat delays.
> 
> 
> ---
> 
> 
> ## 🧪 IV. ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA & UAT VERIFICATION

#### 📋 STRY-0627-JOLLY-ROGER-STREAM: 🛡️ SOVEREIGNOS: IPTV 'JOLLY ROGER' HLS STREAM SANDBOX & GLOBAL OVERLAY ENGINE
*   **System ID:** `10c1ada8193c46fa9e8810149b0b7eaa`
*   **Full Description:**
> ﻿# 🛡️ SOVEREIGNOS: IPTV 'JOLLY ROGER' HLS STREAM SANDBOX & GLOBAL OVERLAY ENGINE
> 
> 
> **Ticket ID:** STRY-0627-JOLLY-ROGER-STREAM (WO-2026-097)  
> **Priority:** ⚡ P1 — High-Priority Operations  
> **Assigned To:** `antigravity`  
> **Ecosystem Location:** Sovereign Sports Main Stack (Port 3016, `/home/james/SovereignOS/01_Sovereign_Portal/src/components/IPTVPlayer.tsx`)  
> **Verification Node:** Clio (100.73.155.70)  
> **Parent Sprint:** Sovereign Sports Telemetry & IPTV (Sprint ID 3)
> 
> 
> ---
> 
> 
> ## 📌 I. THE PILOT'S DIRECTIVE & SITUATION OVERVIEW
> 
> 
> Yo, Pilot! We are raising the Jolly Roger on the high seas to capture and embed live match streams (IPTV/HLS) directly into our three-pane Sovereign Sports command center. Relying on standard web-embedded players locks us out or floods our browser with malicious scripts and ads. We need an isolated, sandboxed HLS.js video player that can cleanly stream `.m3u8` playlists alongside our live telemetry diamond and the Cross Talk chat lounge.
> 
> 
> Furthermore, we are decoupling our overlay triggers from game-specific variables. We are building a robust library of **Global Dynamic Overlays** (Max Hype, Chaos/Disciplinary, Absolute Heartbreaker) that respond to raw, universal telemetry thresholds. These overlays can be fired passively by our TMI Engine or manually overridden on-demand using the laptop/mobile Producer console.
> 
> 
> ---
> 
> 
> ## ⚙️ II. TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS & IMPLEMENTATION STEPS
> 
> 
> Antigravity must execute the following operations on the Clio core codebase:
> 
> 
> ### 1. Sandboxed HLS.js IPTV Player (`IPTVPlayer.tsx`)
> Create a highly secure, responsive React video player component at `/home/james/SovereignOS/01_Sovereign_Portal/src/components/IPTVPlayer.tsx`:
> *   Import `hls.js` natively to parse live `.m3u8` playlist feeds.
